Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the statistical_mechanics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 429. The moduli space has dimension at most 878 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 429 + O(1/n^878). Summing over all modes and applying the statistical_mechanics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the statistical_mechanics framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 498. The resulting operator achieves bound O(243/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on statistical_mechanics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 498. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 243. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)
